	end;

				mes needtr("우리는 주었다", "We gave");
				break;
			case 4:
				mes needtr("유혹적인 붉은 입술에게", "To the seducing red lips");
				break;
			case 5:
				mes needtr("그 유혹적인 붉은 입술", "Those seductive red lips");
				set .@point,.@point + 10;
				break;
			}
			next;
			if (.@point > 40) {
				hideoffnpc "Dvalin#1";
				mes needtr("[드발린]", "[Dvalin]");
				mes "...?";
				mes needtr("알프릭?", "Alfrik?");
				mes needtr("당신인가요?", "Is that you?");
				close;
			}
			close;
		case 4:
			mes "["+ strcharinfo(0) +"]";
			mes needtr("^333333*콜록콜록!*", "^333333*Cough cough!*");
			mes needtr("아악!", "Aaaack!");
			mes needtr("먼지가 너무 많아요!", "It's so dusty!");
			close;
		}
	}
	else {
		mes needtr("^3355FF그냥 평범한", "^3355FFJust a normal");
		mes needtr("이끼로 덮인 바위.^000000", "rock, covered in moss.^000000");
		close;
	}

OnTouch_:
	if (god_brising > 41) {
		emotion ET_SURPRISE, playerattached();
	}
	end;
}

OnTouch_:
	if ($God2 < $@god_check1) {
		mes "[" + strcharinfo(0) + "]";
		mes needtr("에휴, 어쩔 수 없네.", "Beh, no way out.");

		close;

		stopnpctimer;
		startnpctimer;
		close;
	}
	else {
		warp "mjo_dun02",118,56;
	}
	end;

OnTimer1000:
	mapannouncelang "mjo_dun02","그레르: 어서 내가 널 믿을 수 있다는 걸 보여줘!", "Grer: Hurry and show that I can trust you!",0,0x70DBDB;
